# JOURNAL — sera
Continuity ledger per CTO charter §6/§6bis. Every entry is evidence-grounded.

Format per entry:
## <date> · <slice/WO id> · <status: in-progress | in-review | done | blocked-on-founder>
- What was done (with the tool result / test output that proves it)
- Decisions made · safest-defaults applied on open ⏳ (flagged) · founder overrides
- Pending / next

## 2026-08-06 · AUDIT-B+1 F12 — the copy-lint learns administrative register, and the pin is GATED · DONE (branch)
- **What changed here:** `@platform/i18n` re-pinned to `199bc2ab` (canon), plus a new negative fixture `catalog.administrative-register.json` wired into the gate board.
- **Why the pin alone was not enough (verifier round 2 blocker).** Canon carried the new word list while every app still resolved the OLD package, so the lint was unchanged for users and the journal said DONE. The pin lives in **two places per repo** — the root `package.json` AND each workspace package's own; moving only the root reports success and changes nothing.
- **Why the fixture (verifier round 3 blocker).** The existing negative fixture violates « Veuillez » and « séquestre », which are in BOTH the old and new lists — so it discriminates nothing, and a pin regression left the board GREEN while the bureaucratic escape passed again. **Proven by execution.** The new fixture violates ONLY a token F12 added, so reverting the pin makes it stop failing and turns the board RED. §4: enforced by construction, not by discipline.
- **The list RETREATED on evidence before shipping.** A verifier found honest sentences the first list refused — « Pas de formalité compliquée. », « Sans formalités : votre boutique est prête. », « Suivez l'instruction du livreur. » Negation copy is the promise this platform makes to an informal-sector seller. `formalité(s)`, `procédure(s)` and bare `instruction` were REMOVED; the escape is caught by the phrase « cours d'instruction » instead. Those four sentences are now CONTROLS in the canon tests.
- **Evidence:** every catalog in this repo lints clean through the newly installed package; gate board green with the new negative fixture failing for the right reason (« cours d'instruction »).
- **⚠ MERGE ORDER:** the pin references a `platform-contracts` commit that is on the branch only. **platform-contracts merges to main BEFORE this repo does**, or the pin dangles.

## 2026-07-10 · CI incident · closed (founder-authorized direct-to-main fixes)
- **Symptom → root cause, four layers deep (all figures log-copied from Actions runs):** main went red at the WO-1.2 merge (runs 29066791600, 29066802882). Layer 1: the merged lockfile carried pnpm-mangled scp-form git-dep URLs (`git@github.com:…`) → runner has no SSH key → `Permission denied (publickey)`. Layer 2 (my error, `f5ef408`): I added SSH-form insteadOf rewrites with plain `git config --global` — but `url.<base>.insteadOf` is a **single-valued key**, so each write silently **overwrote** the working https rewrite; only the last (`ssh://` form) survived. Layer 3 (`c871690`): lockfile URLs fixed to https form — run 29067960965 still red, now `fatal: could not read Username for 'https://github.com'` (anonymous fetch: the https rewrite no longer existed). Layer 4 (diagnostic run 29068268032, temporary step `cba8f8c`): `token length: 93 · prefix: github_pat_` · `API probe: 200` (token valid, repo accessible) · `git ls-remote via insteadOf: FAILED` — token fine, rewrite dead. Root cause **reproduced locally**: after the three plain writes the gitconfig held exactly one insteadOf (the last); with `--add`, all three forms rewrite (localhost-probe shows the rewritten URL attempted for https, scp, and ssh:// forms alike).
- **Fix `297d5d5`:** `--add` on the second and third insteadOf entries. **Run 29069416200 (run #7): SUCCESS** — install 27s, 15/15 turbo tasks, Playwright 2/2, "ALL GATES GREEN (positives passed; every negative fixture failed as required)".
- **This commit:** removes the temporary diagnostic step (its own comment: "Remove when green") + this incident record.
- **Cross-repo state (read-only checks at origin/main):** boutik-plus already uses the correct `--add` form (`317a85e`, its own earlier incident — "sera carries the same class" was right). shop-plus carries the same plain-set SSH-forms step at the END of its job (after install/gates), so it is currently harmless dead weight — its CI is green (run 6/6 success) — but it overwrites the https rewrite in the runner's final gitconfig and will break the build if anything needing platform-contracts auth is ever added after it, or if the step is moved up. **Flagged for the founder: one-line fix (--add + move above install or delete), not applied — outside the sera authorization.**
- **Follow-up ③ (lockfile scp-URL watch) closed by this incident;** standing guard: lockfile git-dep URLs stay https-form (`git+https://github.com/…` + `resolution.repo` https), and any multi-entry insteadOf in CI uses `--add` from the second entry on.

## 2026-07-10 · CI incident #2 · closed (founder report: red after the WO-1.3 merge; clean-store reproduction, all figures log-copied)
- **Reproduced locally, runner-cold:** pristine clone at `ca90d55`, fresh pnpm store/cache, ci.yml auth rewrites applied — `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` → `ERR_PNPM_BROKEN_LOCKFILE — duplicated mapping key (1151:3)`, exit 1. The first workflow step after auth fails on every sera run since the merge. Invisible to every local gate run: `run-gates.sh` starts at typecheck on existing node_modules; CI is the only path that parses the lockfile cold.
- **Root cause (my error, `89e3726`):** at `d88ec76` the lockfile carried BOTH ssh-form and https-form keys for four `@platform` git deps — distinct keys, parseable. The NB-5 16-line URL rewrite converted the ssh-form key lines to https, making them byte-identical to the https keys already present → duplicated mapping keys for certification/contracts/kernel-types/ui-tokens in `packages:` AND `snapshots:` (8 duplicate blocks). **The NB-5 record's "frozen install green" claim was false of the committed file** — proven this session: the `89e3726` lockfile fails frozen install with the identical error, warm or cold (the parse error is deterministic). That verification was never run against the committed file. Standing rule tightened: a lockfile-touching commit's install proof runs against the exact bytes being committed, immediately before commit.
- **Fix (this commit):** dedupe — script-removed the second copy of each of the 8 adjacent byte-identical duplicate blocks (34 deletions, 0 insertions; identity asserted before removal). Post-fix key count 2 per package (packages + snapshots).
- **Post-fix clean-store reproduction GREEN in workflow order:** install exit 0 in 29.9s (CI green-run parity: 27s) with dist PRESENT for all five `@platform` git deps on the fresh store · `pnpm build` 7/7 tasks · `run-gates.sh` exit 0, "ALL GATES GREEN (positives passed; every negative fixture failed as required)". Fixed lockfile sha256-identical between the validated cold clone and this commit (`09d3fa6a…`).
- **Candidate classes cleared with evidence:** (a) `onlyBuiltDependencies` carries `@platform/certification`; dist present on fresh store ✓ (b) zero `git+ssh`/scp URLs in the lockfile ✓ (c) drift-check `--pinned-version 0.3.0` matches pin `9308c641` (v0.3.0) ✓ (d) zero wall-clock timing assertions in tests ✓. Auth rewrite forms probed: all three URL forms matched and routed https under the ci.yml config (dummy-token probe; token validity already proven by green run 29069416200).
- **Closure is the founder's Actions tab** — this reproduction is local; the runner's own green run confirms.

## 2026-07-11 · CI INCIDENT #3 (expo-preview red on its first armed runs) · fixed on main `32792a8`
- **Both armed `expo-preview` runs were red** (this repo ×2 — the founder's screenshot; boutik ×4, shop ×2 — one shared defect). Diagnosis from the complete Actions logs (pulled via API, log-copied): token check, checkout, install, `eas init --non-interactive --force` (`✔ Project successfully linked (ID: e0768e18-1ea3-48e0-82da-cbdf86f41f84)`) and `update:configure` (`✔ Configured updates.url`) all PASSED — the failure is the third command's bundling: `SyntaxError: index.ts: Cannot find module 'babel-preset-expo'`, identical across the three repos. The token and the founder's account are NOT the cause; the founder's capture ended above the terminal error line.
- **Root cause (config-class, reproduced in boutik's cold clone, byte-identical error):** `babel.config.js` names `babel-preset-expo`; the app manifest never declared it; pnpm strict isolation leaves it unresolvable from the app directory on a fresh install; babel has no fallback (direct `@babel/core` transform emulation).
- **Why WO-4.0's evidence missed it (failure-mode #8, named):** the `expo start --offline` boot probe transforms nothing until a client connects, and local exports silently reused Metro's cross-directory content-hash cache (`$TMPDIR/metro-cache`), bypassing babel. Standing lesson: bundling evidence = a cache-isolated `expo export`, never a dev-server boot line.
- **Fix `32792a8`:** `babel-preset-expo ~13.2.5` (expo 53.0.27's own declared range) added to `apps/rider-app` devDependencies; lockfile by regeneration (+3 lines, zero new packages, dup-key assert 0). Evidence: resolve-from-app-dir OK · gates exit 0 (ALL GATES GREEN) · cache-isolated export exit 0 (579 modules bundled) · boutik cold-clone proof of the identical fix passed. The eas-cli non-pin ruling stands (`eas-cli@latest` = 20.5.1 in the red runs; init/configure worked under it — the non-pin is NOT the cause; pins at first founder-verified publish).
- **The founder's next expo-preview runs are the only closure** — this entry claims the local reproduction and fix, never the publish.

## 2026-07-11 · WO-4.1 (LE MONDE NAVIGABLE, rider-app) · in review — branch `e4/wo-4.1`
- **Founder feedback driving this WO, verbatim:** "i thought it was going to be like a real app where i can scroll and see all the functionalities." Principle held: **no new business capability** — the WO-1.2/1.3/2.2/2.4 rider flows became REACHABLE; `custody-flow.ts` and everything under `services/` are byte-untouched (verifier-confirmed 0-line diffs).
- **Commit `12dad14` (build, subagent — diff reviewed line-by-line at maximum rigor, custody path):** journey as DATA (`src/journey.ts`, 17 screens), with **rule-owned edges COMPUTED from `custody-flow.ts` over full input domains** (both connectivity branches with the WO-2.4 door-precedes-drop mapping · both payment modes · both door signals with pending mapping to itself · all 7 failure reasons) — the map never re-encodes a transition, and the spine test asserts edge-set identity with the functions. Demo store: every custody move guarded by `expectStep` (out-of-order throws); checklist gate = EVERY policy check or no seal; refusal at verification closes with dignity; offline branch parks at `evidence_pending` with the drop LOCKED; exactly ONE door-advance surface (`applyProviderDoorSignal` — rider-asserted values refused by type AND at runtime, SE-I11). 4 seeded « (démo) » courses: the full happy walk (Awa) · a 2e passage with visible lineage (Salif) · a buyer-fault return (Mariam, seed reason test-pinned to really escalate) · the offline branch (Issouf). Reschedule expiry SPAWNS the honest 2e-passage course (attempt 2, lineage), mirroring boutik's honest-walk pattern.
- **Two safest-defaults, flagged ⚠ for the founder (demo-level, rule source silent — not rule changes):** ① `retryDelivery` routes `retry_window → door_inspection`, where the OLD App.tsx (5efef1c:246) retried straight to `'drop'` — in Option-B that could surface the drop code without the payment leg of the attempt; the new route keeps the drop code LAST behind the provider-confirmed payment (SE-I11 conservative reading; verified against the old source myself). ② In-course « Retour » semantics — see the fix chain below; final rule: **course → liste → accueil, a two-level waypoint ladder; no stack pop exists.**
- **Verifier round 1 (initial), verdict verbatim: `VERDICT: FAIL`** — blocking: "un-guarded « Retour » leaves stale custody screens whose primary action throws an unhandled exception (app crash), reachable in 5 taps inside the documented happy walk … with a course at `refusal_reason`, back re-shows the **drop screen offering « Valider le code »** mid-refusal-ladder — exactly the 'screen that lies about custody' the safest-default claims to prevent." (Custody itself never at risk — the store refused every stale move.) **Fix `c8a5004`:** `COURSE_BACK_STEPS` = every journey screen except service/courses; in-course back → course list, state kept.
- **Verifier round 2 (delta), verdict verbatim: `VERDICT: FAIL`** — blocking: "push-then-pop still reaches a stale course screen with a throwing primary action … five in-screen « Retour aux courses » buttons **push** `'courses'` via `go()` … header « ← Retour » on the list … reveals the stale `retour_colis` screen → press « Échanger les deux codes » again → throws … ~6 taps." **Fix `ecbf32c`:** back() is a TOTAL three-arm rule with **no pop arm** (course screen → list · list → home · root → nothing); all five in-course « Retour aux courses » reset via `toCourses`; `go('courses')` occurs exactly once (home screen). Spine test pins: single `go('courses')` · toCourses shape · absence of `s.slice(0, -1)` · the list-goes-home arm.
- **Verifier round 3 (delta 2), verdict verbatim: `VERDICT: PASS`** — "I ran a BFS over the faithful handler set … to depth 12 — 189 distinct reachable stacks, exhaustive with the retry cycle covered twice — asserting three invariants on every state: (A) `'courses'` never sits above any course screen, (B) `'service'` only at the root, (C) `back()` never lands on a course screen. All hold." NBs (accepted, journaled): inert `→ courses` edges remain declared in JOURNEY (no handler uses them; the single-occurrence pin fails CI on any future re-opening — pruning would be cosmetic) · no ladder entry from `payment_wait` (matches the WO-2.4 shell; named for the E2 ladder work) · the payment_wait « confirmed » button feeds the compile-time sandbox constant (honest labels, pending branch buttonless, store rejects non-signal values — the live provider feed lands at assembly) · WALKTHROUGH line-8 phrasing.
- **Evidence at `ecbf32c`:** tests **20/20** (journey-spine 6 · demo-store 9 · shell 3 · preview-banner 2) · typecheck 0 · ALL GATES GREEN · serial isolated dual export exit 0 ×2, **Android 583 · iOS 585** modules · **cold proof green at `ecbf32c`** (clone, DUMMYTOKEN config, frozen install 0, gates 0, isolated android export 0).
- **The 5-second test, per screen:** service — *je prends la route, une action* · courses — *mes courses, leur état honnête* · affectation — *où aller, et que mon accord reste en attente du réseau* · verify — *je vérifie tout, ou je refuse dignement* · refused — *c'est refusé, proprement, et voici la suite* · seal — *je scelle, le colis est sous ma garde* · evidence — *une photo, la preuve accompagne* · evidence_pending — *pas de réseau : en attente, jamais « fait »* · door_inspection — *le client regarde, il accepte ou signale* · payment_wait — *l'argent d'abord — la confirmation vient du réseau, pas de moi* · drop — *le code du client, dernière clé* · delivered — *livré, validé* · refusal_reason — *je dis pourquoi, en mots simples* · retry_window — *un nouvel essai, un temps limité* · refused_final — *arrêt digne, cause dite, frais dits* · reschedule_planned — *rien n'est perdu, un 2e passage arrive* · retour_colis — *le colis rentre, deux codes, ensemble*.
- **Gallery (missing-state law), commit `7363517`:** the RN rider journey named as an explicit gap (the Playwright gallery captures only the dispatch console; the rider journey's visual channel = WALKTHROUGH.md + Expo Go). Re-capture 4/4 states passed twice; the one clock-driven PNG re-encodes a few bytes differently with **pixel-identical** content — committed capture kept, zero visual drift. Note: `run-gates.sh` re-runs the gallery capture, so a gates run can leave that PNG re-encoded in the working tree; discard it before closing commits (it bit once this slice — verifier NB, resolved).
- **Tooling notes (mine to own, cross-WO):** ① cold proofs must use SHORT isolation dirs (`mktemp -d` under /tmp) — a deep scratchpad TMPDIR exceeded chromium's 108-char unix-socket limit and failed every Playwright launch in the first shop/sera cold runs (re-run green; identical .hbc hashes proved the failure environmental). ② the v1 dual-export script's second `mktemp` ran with TMPDIR pointing inside the first iteration's deleted dir → `$ISO` empty → the iOS output landed at the container path `/dist-ios` (isolation intent held — the dir was fresh by construction; counts/exits valid). Script fixed (v2 allocates both dirs up front); the stray `/dist-ios` (one sandbox bundle, no repo reference — verified by grep across all four repos) awaits an approved standalone removal.

## 2026-07-12 · WO-6.2 · in-review (expo-preview publish was RED — the rider's face off the phone; do NOT merge)
- **Branch `e6/wo-6.2`** off sera `main` (contains `6644310`). Commits: `282cf55` fix · `107ac6b` regression guard + allowlist · closing commit (this entry + packet + verifier verdict). DIAGNOSE-FIRST work order.
- **The failing run, verbatim (CI job 86695010823, run 29209620504):** `.../expo/bin/cli config --json exited with non-zero code: 1` · `Error: project:init command failed.` Runs #21 (`6644310`) and #22 (`4e1b278`) FAILED; **run #20 (`b705251`, the commit BEFORE the WO-6.1 merge) SUCCEEDED** — clean before/after, WO-6.1 broke the publish. eas-cli surfaces only the exit code; expo config's own stderr is captured.
- **The REAL cause, reproduced in a cache-isolated cold clone (raw invocation, exactly as eas spawns expo):** `PluginError: Failed to resolve plugin for module "expo-font" relative to ".../apps/rider-app"` at `@expo/config-plugins/.../plugin-resolver.js:96` → `withConfigPlugins` → `getConfig`. **CAUSE = suspect (a): the expo-font config plugin.** WO-6.1 added the plugin to `app.json` but expo-font was only a TRANSITIVE dep; `eas init` spawns expo's cli RAW (no pnpm env), so `@expo/config-plugins` does plain node resolution from `apps/rider-app`, where a transitive dep is unreachable under pnpm's isolated node_modules → PluginError. `pnpm exec expo config` MASKS it (pnpm injects resolution paths); typecheck/vitest never run `expo config` — so nothing local caught it.
- **Suspects eliminated against the evidence:** (b) fonts excluded from bundle — the failure is at plugin *resolution* (config eval), upstream of any asset bundling, and names the plugin module not a font; the 7 .ttf are tracked. (c) lockfile/frozen mismatch — CI's frozen install logged "Done in 57.2s"; my cold frozen install succeeded. (d) native rebuild (react-native-svg/expo-haptics) — the stack is `@expo/config-plugins`; no native build ran; only expo-font has a plugin in app.json.
- **Fix (`282cf55`):** declare `expo-font` (`~14.0.12`, the SDK-54-aligned version already resolved transitively) as a DIRECT dependency of `apps/rider-app`; lockfile regenerated (+3 lines). **Forcing evidence:** the raw `expo config --json` (as eas does) was EXIT 1 (PluginError) before the fix, **EXIT 0 with 5 Archivo fonts in the resolved config** after — proven in a cache-isolated cold clone. **RULING ② intact:** direct-dep addition only; `app.json` keeps the expo-font config plugin (native embedding, font in the binary at first frame); `App.tsx` has no `useFonts`/`loadAsync`/render gate. NOT async loading — no founder decision needed (WO §2 satisfied without a STOP).
- **Regression guard (`107ac6b`):** `apps/rider-app/test/preview-font-embedding.test.ts` — a permanent check that FAILS on all three silent typeface-loss modes: (1) expo-font drops back to transitive (`require.resolve` from the app dir throws — the exact bug), (2) the config plugin removed from app.json, (3) a referenced .ttf deleted/empty. **Proven NON-VACUOUS:** removing a font → "font asset missing"; removing the dep → "expo-font must be a DIRECT dep"; restore → green. The `grand-teint.test.ts` dependency-allowlist bumped 2→3 to admit expo-font (WO-6.2-grounded — the package backing WO-6.1's plugin, required for RULING ②, not a new capability).
- **Evidence (my runs):** rider **58/58** (55 + 3 guard) · typecheck 0 both apps · **`run-gates.sh` EXIT 0** (warm + cold) · **cold proof** @ `107ac6b` — fresh `--local` clone, frozen install, raw expo config EXIT 0 (5 Archivo), ALL GATES GREEN.
- **Verifier (fresh-context, MANDATORY) — VERDICT: PASS, BLOCKING: none** (full text verbatim in `_review/WO-6.2/verifier-verdict.md`). All four mandate items by its own hands: CI log + run history re-read independently (with the honest note that eas-cli swallows the raw PluginError — attribution rests on the definitive local reproduction); **causation confirmed by isolation** (reverting ONLY package.json + lockfile to origin/main reproduced the PluginError, restoring → exit 0 with 5 Archivo — the only change between fail and pass, so the named cause IS the cause); fix confirmed a direct-dep addition NOT an async switch (RULING ② intact, App.tsx zero async-font symbols); the guard bit 3 planted probes (empty font · removed dep · removed plugin) and reverted each; no forbidden touch (diff = 4 files, no workflow change, the failing step NOT disabled).
- **FORBIDDEN respected:** RULING ② not weakened (no async load) · no journey/custody/money semantics touched · the fix does NOT disable the failing step (the workflow is unchanged; the publish now succeeds by resolving the plugin).
- **What only the founder's environment proves:** the true GREEN publish is a live EAS run on the armed `EXPO_TOKEN`, which only a push to `main` triggers. The fix removes the PluginError that blocked it; the on-phone confirmation (Archivo at first frame, no flash) remains the founder's device glance.

## 2026-07-13 · WO-6.4 · HELD by CTO — four questions answered (item 2 re-tiered 🔴 RED, CTO-assigned)
- **Founder HELD the slice.** Item 2 (SOS ack path) re-tiered 🔴 RED and CTO-assigned — NOT mine to close. Below: honest answers, code/journal corrections where asked, and the two items explicitly LEFT OPEN.
- **Q1 (RED) — "a throw is not a state; what does the rider SEE?" NAMED DEFECT, NOT closed.** Traced by hand: the rider ack affordance (`SosSandboxAck`, `kit.tsx`) renders ONLY for the `raised`/`escalated` sheet state; its only caller `sosSandboxAck` (`App.tsx:324`) passes `world.incident.responder` and has **no try/catch**. The console ack (`main.ts`) is the same shape (`acknowledgeSos(inc, inc.responder)`, button gated by `canAcknowledge`). Consequence: **the responder-match guard is UNREACHABLE from both UIs today** — a mismatch cannot originate from either human's hand, so there is currently **no rider-facing screen for it** (not « en attente », not an honest refusal). BUT if the guard ever fires (a future real dispatch/network response crediting the wrong responder, or a miswired caller), the throw propagates out of the click handler, the `setSos('acknowledged')` never runs, and the human **sees NOTHING** (dev: an uncaught error). **The guard protects the DATA layer only; the HUMAN layer has no designed honest state for a rejected safety ack — that is the defect, correctly RED.** Left OPEN for the CTO-assigned design (an honest « accusé refusé / réessayer », never a generic « échec », never nothing). Not built here.
- **Q2 — item 1 is a STATUS, not a PROOF. Corrected + NAMED DEBT.** "publish-preview success" proves the EAS publish RAN; it does NOT prove five Archivo weights render as five faces (staged ≠ consumed — the exact class of bug WO-5.1 was, caught only on-device). Item 1 stands ONLY as a status (publish green, run #26). **The font-renders-as-5-distinct-faces correctness is a NAMED DEBT riding to the device-matrix pass.** Strongest evidence available is `apps/rider-app/test/font-embedding.test.ts` — a pure-TS sfnt reader asserting the FIVE COMMITTED TTFs carry five distinct name tables + weight classes (green) — but that is SOURCE/staged bytes, **necessary but NOT sufficient** (only parsing the PUBLISHED bytes, or a device screenshot of five distinct weights, closes it). Not closed here.
- **Q3 — name the consumer. Answer: NOTHING consumes these bytes for comparison.** Confirmed (and the verifier independently confirmed): no `toHaveScreenshot`/snapshot/baseline anywhere, `gallery.spec.ts` asserts capture SUCCESS, `build-gallery.mjs` asserts EXISTENCE, no PNG is tracked. **So the WO-4.1 landmine is gone because the COMPARISON is gone, not because the bytes are stable — and the `console-course-remise` AA flip is therefore irrelevant.** Corrected `gallery.spec.ts` + `.gitignore` comments and this ledger to say exactly that; dropped "byte-stable" as a headline claim (the two-run diff was DIAGNOSTIC — to answer the WO's "prove it" and to learn whether determinism was achievable — not a property any consumer relies on). Kept the fixed clock ONLY because it makes the gallery's VISIBLE content deterministic for the founder's eye (a lease HH:MM from the fixed demo time, not the capture minute), not for byte-equality — will revert on a CTO ruling.
- **Q4 — base vs CURRENT origin/main. Ran it:** `git fetch` then `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/main e6/wo-6.4` → **TRUE**. `origin/main` is still `3c78316a880a959009b92debc2355249e3d52aae` (unchanged since the fork); `git log origin/main ^e6/wo-6.4` → **zero delta commits**; fork point == origin/main. ## 2026-07-13 · STANDING LAW — THE COLD-PROOF LAW, AMENDED (founder ruling 2026-07-13, verbatim)
> A cold proof isolates the CACHE and shows the AUTH.
> - COLD: `export HOME=<fresh empty dir>` — pnpm store, XDG caches, all state cold; every dep genuinely re-fetched.
> - PERMITTED, and SHOWN in the artifact: re-injecting the CI-equivalent credential/proxy rewrite for the canonical `https://` form. Auth is config, not cache; no machine anywhere installs a private dep with zero credentials.
> - FORBIDDEN: any rewrite that CHANGES THE URL FORM the committed lockfile resolves (`git@host:` → anything). That is the shop-plus defect class — a form-fix masquerading as auth. The lockfile-url-form gate independently asserts nothing exists to mask.
>
> The artifact must show BOTH lines from now on, all lanes.
- Sera's WO-6.9-b cold proof already complies: the re-injected rewrite is `https://github.com/ → http://***@127.0.0.1:.../git/` (auth for the canonical https:// form the lockfile resolves), NOT a `git@host:` form-change; the `no-ssh-lockfile-urls` gate independently proves 0 ssh-form URLs. Both COLD (fresh HOME) and AUTH (rewrite) lines are shown in `_review/WO-6.9-b/cold-gates.log`.

## 2026-07-14 · SERA-S2 · in-review (🔴 custody evidence on the outbox — SE-I06 finality server-ack-gated; do NOT merge)
- **Branch `e6/sera-s2-custody-outbox`** off the merged S3 head (`f4aadfe`, canon v0.9.8). Read-before-write grounded: **SE-I06** (`docs/Sera-Build-Spec.md:38` — « Offline evidence may be queued, but **custody/delivery validation + financial release remain pending until authoritative server ack** »), the whole custody walk in `demo/store.ts` + `custody-flow.ts`, the SE-I11 `payment_wait` pattern it mirrors (the rider CANNOT self-assert an external confirmation), and the S1/S3 outbox durable-write pattern (`outbox.ts`/`sos.ts`).
- **RED-PROOF FIRST (`_review/SERA-S2/red-proof.log`).** The retired `nextAfterEvidence(connectivity)` returned `'drop'` when **online** — unlocking on connectivity, conflating "online" with "acked". The red-proof asserted online capture must stay `'evidence_pending'`; against the pre-fix code it **FAILED** (`expected 'door_inspection' to be 'evidence_pending'`) — proving the server-ack gate is load-bearing.
- **THE FIX — finality waits for the ACK, not for online.** `nextAfterEvidence` is **retired**; `custody-flow.ts` gains `stepAfterEvidenceAck(ack: FlushOutcome)` (`applied`/`idempotentReplay` → `'drop'` → mapped to `door_inspection` per the WO-2.4 door-before-drop mapping; `collision-refused` → stays `'evidence_pending'`, surfaced, never a silent unlock) + the sandbox constant `SANDBOX_EVIDENCE_ACK='applied'` (typed data like `SANDBOX_DOOR_SIGNAL`; the live outbox flush drives it at assembly). `captureEvidence(world, id)` **drops the connectivity param** and unconditionally lands `evidence_pending` (LOCKS the drop, online or offline); `applyEvidenceServerAck(world, id, ack)` is the **only** exit (guards `['evidence_pending']`), mirroring `applyProviderDoorSignal`/SE-I11.
- **THE CAPTURE RIDES THE OUTBOX (`src/offline/evidence.ts`, mirrors `sos.ts`).** `EVIDENCE_KIND='delivery.evidence'`, `appendEvidence(store, commandId, intent)` persists `{commandId, kind, payload, status:'pending'}` — command_id minted **once** at the gesture, survives kill+reboot, flushed at-least-once with dedup so it applies EXACTLY once. `App.tsx`: the evidence capture mints once, lands `evidence_pending` instantly, then `void appendEvidence(outboxStore, …)` in the background; `sosStore`→**one** `outboxStore` (a single document-dir queue for both rider write kinds, entries discriminated by `kind`). The `evidence_pending` screen mirrors `payment_wait` — the rider cannot assert the ack; the sandbox constant stands in; offline shows only the wait.
- **JOURNEY GRAPH (rule-computed, not re-encoded).** `evidence → ['evidence_pending']`; `evidence_pending` forward edge computed from `stepAfterEvidenceAck` over `applied`/`collision-refused` → `['door_inspection','courses']`. Spine BFS reachability + no-dangling still asserted.
- **FIXTURES.** `test/evidence-finality.test.ts` **5/5** (the red-proof grown): capture LOCKS the drop (`validateDropCode` throws while pending) · `collision-refused` does NOT unlock · `applied`/`idempotentReplay` unlock through `door_inspection` to `delivered` · ack cannot precede a capture. `test/evidence-outbox.test.ts` **3/3** (mirrors sos-outbox): offline-confirm-survives-reboot · evidence-flush-emits-exactly-one (applied once, replay after, `restore` drops) · id-stable-across-retry. `demo-store.test.ts` happy/offline/expiry updated to walk through the ack; `journey-spine.test.ts` rule-owned edges updated. **Rider-app 86/86.**
- **3 catalog strings** (register-tagged, French-Voice clean; `copy-lint OK: 145 entries, 0 violations`): `evidence.pending` refined to the SE-I06 truth (« La photo est partie. La remise attend la confirmation de Séra. » — waits for the confirmation, not merely the network) · `evidence.confirmed_status` « Séra a confirmé la photo. » · `evidence.continue_action` « Continuer ».

## 2026-07-19 · FOLLOW-ON (NAMED, UNBUILT) · RELAIS-REAL-BACKING — masked-relay telephony backend
- **Status: UNBUILT. Record only (founder ruling — « no build, just the record »).** The RELAIS affordance shipped in WO-relais (main `53e0e3d`) is a **CERTIFIED MOCK**: `calling` is a local UI toggle (`relaisFor().onToggle`, App.tsx:493 → `RelaisRow`, faso-kit.tsx:151). No call is placed, no number is dialed, no number is exposed (guard `faso-relais.test.ts`: zero `Linking`, zero `tel:`, no number literal).
- **The honesty caveat, on the record:** « les deux numéros restent privés » holds **today only because nothing dials** — it is vacuously true (there is no number in flight to leak). The instant it is backed by real telephony, that line stops being vacuous and becomes a **guarantee the provider must enforce** (both legs — rider→proxy and buyer→proxy — masked, neither real MSISDN ever revealed). Until then the copy is safe strictly by the absence of a call.
- **Where the real provider plugs in (the seam):** `relaisFor().onToggle` (App.tsx) is the single seam. Today it flips local `calling`. Real backing replaces it with a **provider call-initiation**: request a masked-call/relay session from a chosen provider → the provider bridges rider↔buyer through a **proxy number**, both real numbers hidden. `RelaisRow`'s single `calling` boolean then becomes a real **session state** (requesting · ringing · connected · ended · failed) reflecting the live relay, not a toggle. The « privés » copy must be **gated on the provider actually masking** (shown only on a provider-confirmed masked session), never asserted client-side.
- **Contract-certification debt (Execution Contract §3 — the mock may not hide real timing/failure):** the current toggle is instant and always-succeeds. A real relay has **latency and failure modes** (provider-down, no-answer, call-failed, number-unreachable, rate/spend caps). The real backing MUST model those as honest states (queued/offline included — a relay call is never « done » offline). Flagged so the mock is not mistaken for full-fidelity integration (failure-mode #8).
- **Milestone shape: E3-SHAPED, NOT E3-INDEPENDENT.** Provider + number-masking is **carrier/KYC territory** — external, regulated, provider-selection + compliance gated, the same class as the payment-provider integration. It rides the **E3 provider-reconciliation / real-provider milestone**; it does NOT start as a standalone slice and does NOT open until that milestone opens (chosen masked-call provider + carrier/KYC compliance in hand). No custody/money/journey coupling — it is a rider-comfort/privacy channel, not a custody or payment path.
- **Scope of THIS entry:** documentation only. No code, no gate, no dependency added. The shipped affordance stands as-is (a certified, honest mock) until RELAIS-REAL-BACKING opens at E3.

## 2026-08-06 · SE-LIVE-1 · the logistics Worker DOOR — one durable authority, three doors, deploy workflow
- **Why this slice (founder ruling):** Cercle refused at the SP9 gate (0/10); founder chose « build the whole Séra », with the standing directive **Séra = simple** (few riders, five-screen app, no fleet apparatus). Séra was library-complete but deployment-absent: every core tested, NOTHING durable, NOTHING deployed, the one worker entry (`worker/assignment-lease-do.ts`) **unauthenticated**. SE-LIVE-1 puts the logistics authority on a real Worker behind real doors.
- **Grounded first (read-before-write, in full):** ready-queue.ts · rider-registry.ts · assignment-lease.ts (`decideLease` command contract) · manual-assignment.ts · leased-assignment.ts (LeasedDispatch + witness) · reschedule.ts · the old DO + both e2e suites · boutik's supplier-code mint/hash block (fulfillment-do.ts) · platform's protection-service door/CORS/provenance + deploy workflow.
- **Built — `worker/logistics-do.ts` (LogisticsDO, idFromName `'logistique'`):** ONE durable object composing the four tested cores; the whole grant decision (SE1.1 recheck + assignability + atomic acquire + witnessed book entry) runs in one workerd-serialized place. **No law added:** the DO loads snapshots, routes into the cores, persists snapshots. Additive `snapshot()/restore()` on ReadyQueue/RiderRegistry/AssignmentBook/GrantedLeaseWitness (witness restored EXPLICITLY, never inferred from lease state). On a handler throw the DO drops in-memory state and reloads from the last durable truth (no half-applied memory outliving its persist).
- **`worker/index.ts` — three doors:** OPS (`SERA_OPS_SECRET`): riders/certify/code mint·revoke·inventory, `/ops/board`, `/ops/assign`, `/ops/expire-due`, + the preserved raw `POST /authority/dispatch`. INTAKE (`SERA_INTAKE_SECRET`, its own key — neither door opens the other, proven in tests): `/intake/task-ready` + `/intake/funding` + `/intake/readiness` facts. RIDER (personal codes, boutik pattern: SHA-256 hash IS the lookup key, mint refuses for unknown riders, one uniform 401): `/rider/moi`, ack-privacy, shift start/end, assignment ack/decline with an **ownership check** (foreign id ≡ unknown id — no oracle). HMAC timing-safe compare + fail-closed empty-secret + exact-origin CORS (`SERA_CONSOLE_ORIGIN`) mirrored from protection-service. `/health` carries the F9 provenance stamp (`__SERA_RELEASE__`/`__SERA_CANON__`).
- **FAIL-CLOSED PROJECTIONS (the deploy-order law):** Séra never computes funding/readiness — it consumes signals. The intake door STORES posted facts per order; an order with no fact reads unknown+stale → the admission gate refuses closed (`funding_projection_stale`). Nothing is admitted until SE-LIVE-2's real producers (Shop+ funding, Boutik+ readiness) post facts through this door — which therefore ships FIRST, correctly. A producer may mark its own fact `stale:true`; Séra never invents freshness.
- **`/authority/dispatch` evolution (settled in-flight, not tidying):** route + wire contract preserved byte-compatibly (200/409/400, only-successes-persisted), now deciding against the SAME lease state as `/ops/assign` (one lease truth) and BEHIND the ops door. Old `assignment-lease-do.ts` deleted; its e2e evolved (same five behavioral assertions + new door tests: naked/wrong bearer = one identical 401, refused command decides nothing). `leased-dispatch.e2e` speaks the same route with auth. The plan's `/dispatch/requeue` was realized as the LAWFUL `/ops/expire-due` sweep — a raw ops requeue would be a second authority; not built.
- **Named debts (open, on the record):** ① no event outbox — pickup.assigned/declined/expired events are returned to callers and durable as records, the emit wire is SE-LIVE-2's seam; ② an ACKED (anchored) assignment has no exit until completion/reschedule land with custody (SE-LIVE-3/5) — a stuck acked rider waits for those slices, acceptable at pilot scale; ③ end-shift custody declaration is honestly `{heldPackageIds: []}` until custody-service is live (the seam that must ask the ledger is marked in-code); ④ rider-door commands are server-confirmed by definition (the offline outbox lives in the app, SE-LIVE-4).
- **Two board fixes with their reasons:** the determinism scan now pins the PURE core only and bans the seedable Math generator in the wrapper (the DO **is** the server boundary now — it stamps instants at the door, fund-do pattern; instants still travel inside commands to the core). `dist-worker/` joined the scanner's EXCLUDED_DIRS + .gitignore, and WO-4.3's committed `assignment-worker.mjs` was untracked: the new bundle inlines the PINNED canon's runtime Zod schemas, so scanning it re-scans vetted canon vocabulary (`balance: FcfaSchema` tripped no-funds) — same rationale as node_modules; the bundle's only other input is repo source, which IS scanned.
- **`logistics-deploy.yml`** (manual-only, protection-deploy mirror): install → build → provenance stamp → EXPLICIT bundle (never a cached task output — the platform turbo-cache lesson) → `wrangler deploy` (+`--var SERA_CONSOLE_ORIGIN` iff set) → BOTH secrets piped via `wrangler secret put` (never arguments, never [vars] — repo is PUBLIC) → F9 poll: live `/health` must answer THIS job's release+canon at `vars.LOGISTICS_BASE`.
- **Evidence (this session's runs):** typecheck 0 · logistics **116/116** (was 94; +20 door e2e incl. 20-concurrent `/ops/assign` → exactly 1 grant; restart-durability on a reused persist dir — riders, codes, queue, assignments, applied-command replay AND lease truth all survive; door separation both ways; phantom-mint refusal; revoke closes the door) · full `run-gates.sh` exit 0 **ALL GATES GREEN**.

## 2026-08-06 · SE-LIVE-1 · verifier round — BLOCKER fixed + MAJOR closed, both pinned mutation-killed
- **Fresh-context verifier ran (standing order 2026-08-05)** on the full diff b766788..5c0c23b with the quoted SE-I01/I02/I06/I08/I09 + SE2.1 text and the slice DoD. It re-ran typecheck/tests/gates itself (116/116, board ALL GREEN, fresh-bundle e2e 38/38) and confirmed DoD items 1–8 with its own evidence. Verdict on 5c0c23b: **NOT MERGEABLE** — one blocker, one major, seven notes.
- **BLOCKER (reproduced by the verifier on workerd, through the ops door): a REPLAYED `/ops/expire-due` with the same `nowIso` re-drove the book with the authority's ORIGINAL expired snapshot.** `expireByTasks` matches by taskId alone, so a task re-granted — and even ACKED — since the first sweep was returned to the queue while the authority kept the anchored lease: task answers `task_already_leased` forever, rider `rider_already_leased` forever, no self-heal. The natural trigger is a timed-out sweep POST retried with the same instant — exactly what the `expire-due-${nowIso}` command-id design invites. Pre-existed in WO-4.3's `LeasedDispatch.expireDue`; this slice put it behind a live door. **FIX:** the orchestrator honors `idempotentReplay` — a replayed sweep returns the historical expired list and drives NEITHER the witness NOR the book (leased-assignment.ts). **Pinned at both levels** (orchestrator e2e + the verifier's exact door probe: sweep → re-grant → ack → replay sweep → the acked rider keeps their course) and **mutation-killed**: with the fix stashed, both pins fail (2 failed | 117 passed); restored, 119/119.
- **MAJOR closed now, not deferred: intake facts were last-write-wins with no ordering** — an at-least-once redelivery of an old `funded` fact after a `cancelled` re-opened admission (SE-I02). **FIX:** both fact routes apply a fact only when its `asOf` is not older than the stored one; an older redelivery is acknowledged (`applied:false, older_fact_ignored`) and never applied. Pinned through the door (funded@T → cancelled@T2 → redeliver funded@T → task-ready still refuses `order_cancelled`).
- **Verifier notes, on the record:** ① the DO-wrapper wall-clock pin was genuinely narrowed (boundary `new Date()` allowed, seedable-Math still banned; pure-core pin intact) — judged sound, but it is a narrowing, not a preservation; ② `/ops/expire-due` accepts an arbitrary instant (founder-gated, deliberate, tests use it); ③ the handler-throw memory-drop protects the NEXT request — an in-flight concurrent request could still persist poisoned state (currently unreachable: every mutation sits in a synchronous block; claim corrected here); ④ raw `/authority/dispatch` lets ops desync book/lease by hand — verified no double-book can result (witness + recheck hold); ⑤ `mintRiderCode` modulo bias ~9/256 vs 8/256 per char — immaterial at ~59 bits, hash-only storage confirmed; ⑥ unauthenticated `/rider/*` probes reach the singleton (one hash + one get each; brute force infeasible, pilot-acceptable); ⑦ the `dist-worker` scanner exclusion verified legitimate (untracked; bundle's non-canon inputs scanned — 28 files; residual force-add exposure identical to the pre-existing `dist/` exclusion).
- **Evidence this round:** typecheck 0 · logistics **119/119** · full board **ALL GATES GREEN** exit 0 · mutation check on the two sweep pins (fail unfixed / pass fixed). NEXT: push → ci.yml green on the new head → targeted re-verify of the fix → ASK the founder.

## 2026-08-06 · SE-LIVE-2c · FOUNDER RULING — he composes the delivery task; the gate still governs
- **THE §7 STOP AND ITS ANSWER.** The canonical `DeliveryTask.location` (pinned contracts, STRICT) requires `pin{lat,lng}` + `zone` + `landmark` + `directions` + `maskedRelay`, all mandatory. Shop+ captures exactly three buyer fields (BC-1a `readBuyerContact`: `phone`, `quartier`, `repere`) — no pin, no directions, no relay; and RELAIS-REAL-BACKING is unbuilt (E3-shaped, journal 2026-07-19). A producer emitting a task would have to INVENT coordinates and a relay id (failure-mode #3). Surfaced with three options; **FOUNDER RULING 2026-08-06: « go with option 1 » — the founder composes the task himself.** No canon change was made, no shape weakened, nothing fabricated.
- **Also recorded, unresolved by this slice:** the canon REQUIRES a pin while Séra's own rider doctrine says « Pas de point GPS… le repère est la navigation » (HANDOFF §4 R8, journal 2026-07-18). Option 1 sidesteps it — the founder supplies a pin he actually knows — but the tension between the canon shape and the product doctrine stands, on the record, for whoever reopens it.
- **Built — `POST /ops/task` (founder's key):** takes the address he can see and NOTHING else, composes the task **through the pinned `DeliveryTaskSchema`** and wraps it in `logistics.task_ready.v1` via `PlatformEventSchema` (actor `ops:sera:fondateur`, correlation `corr-{orderId}`), then feeds it to **the same `onTaskReady`** every producer event goes through. The task id is CSPRNG-minted here, never a caller's claim.
- **THE LINE THIS SLICE DRAWS: he supplies the address, he does NOT skip the law.** An unfunded order refuses 422 `funding_projection_stale`; a funded-but-unprepared order refuses 422 `readiness_projection_stale` — against the founder's own key, with the gate's own reason so he sees WHY. A half-given address (missing pin, non-numeric lat, empty relay, whitespace directions) is 400 — never a task carrying a guessed pin.
- **`GET /ops/a-preparer`:** the orders both producers have vouched for (funded per mode + ready, neither stale) that carry no task yet — derived from the stored facts and the queue, never a count that outlives its evidence.
